{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1782060698251,"sessionID":"ses_114e93f89ffeptWA2a6ndvDVDZ","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_aNcccZznIJPsQiBTeTyR53PG","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/go/pkg/mod/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2@v0.29.0/CHANGELOG.md","offset":131,"limit":80},"output":"<path>/go/pkg/mod/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2@v0.29.0/CHANGELOG.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n131: The v2 preview SDK `v0.25.0` release represents a significant stepping stone bringing the v2 SDK closer to its target design and usability. This release includes significant breaking changes to the v2 preview SDK. The updates in the `v0.25.0` release focus on refactoring and modularization of the SDK’s API clients to use the new [client design](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/438), updated request pipeline (aka [middleware](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/awslabs/smithy-go/middleware)), refactored [credential providers](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials), and [configuration loading](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config) packages.\n132: \n133: We've also bumped the minimum supported Go version with this release. Starting with v0.25.0 the SDK requires a minimum version of Go `v1.15`.\n134: \n135: As a part of the refactoring done to v2 preview SDK some components have not been included in this update. The following is a non exhaustive list of features that are not available.\n136: \n137: * API Paginators - [#439](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/439)\n138: * API Waiters - [#442](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/442)\n139: * Presign URL - [#794](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/794)\n140: * Amazon S3 Upload and Download manager - [#802](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/pull/802)\n141: * Amazon DynamoDB's AttributeValue marshaler, and Expression package - [#790](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/790)\n142: * Debug Logging - [#594](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/594)\n143: \n144: We expect additional breaking changes to the v2 preview SDK in the coming releases. We expect these changes to focus on organizational, naming, and hardening the SDK's design for future feature capabilities after it is released for general availability.\n145: \n146: \n147: #### Relocated Packages\n148: \n149: In this release packages within the SDK were relocated, and in some cases those packages were converted to Go modules. The following is a list of packages have were relocated.\n150: \n151: * `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/external` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config` module\n152: * `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/ec2metadata` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/ec2imds` module\n153: \n154: The `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials` module contains refactored credentials providers.\n155: \n156: * `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/ec2rolecreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/ec2rolecreds`\n157: * `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/endpointcreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/endpointcreds`\n158: * `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/processcreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/processcreds`\n159: * `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/stscreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/stscreds`\n160: \n161: \n162: #### Modularization\n163: \n164: New modules were added to the v2 preview SDK to allow the components to be versioned independently from each other. This allows your application to depend on specific versions of an API client module, and take discrete updates from the SDK core and other API client modules as desired.\n165: \n166: * [github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config)\n167: * [github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials)\n168: * Module for each API client, e.g. [github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3)\n169: \n170: \n171: #### API Clients\n172: \n173: The following is a list of the major changes to the API client modules\n174: \n175: * Removed paginators: we plan to add these back once they are implemented to integrate with the SDK's new API client design.\n176: * Removed waiters: we need to further investigate how the V2 SDK should expose waiters, and how their behavior should be modeled.\n177: * API Clients are now Go modules. When migrating to the v2 preview SDK `v0.25.0`, you'll need to add the API client's module to your application's go.mod file.\n178: * API parameter nested types have been moved to a `types` package within the API client's module, e.g. `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3/types` These types were moved to improve documentation and discovery of the API client, operation, and input/output types. For example Amazon S3's ListObject's operation [ListObjectOutput.Contents](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3/#ListObjectsOutput) input parameter is a slice of [types.Object](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3/types#Object).\n179: * The client operation method has been renamed, removing the `Request` suffix. The method now invokes the operation instead of constructing a request, which needed to be invoked separately. The operation methods were also expanded to include functional options for providing operation specific configuration, such as modifying the request pipeline.\n180: \n181: ```go\n182: result, err := client.Scan(context.TODO(), &dynamodb.ScanInput{\n183:     TableName: aws.String(\"exampleTable\"),\n184: }, func(o *Options) {\n185:     // Limit operation calls to only 1 attempt.\n186:     o.Retryer = retry.AddWithMaxAttempts(o.Retryer, 1)\n187: })\n188: ```\n189: \n190: \n191: #### Configuration\n192: \n193: In addition to the `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/external` package being made a module at `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config`, the `LoadDefaultAWSConfig` function was renamed to `LoadDefaultConfig`.\n194: \n195: The `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/defaults` package has been removed. This release includes significant breaking changes to the v2 preview SDK. The updates in the `v0.25.0` release focus on refactoring and modularization of the SDK’s API clients to use the new [client design](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/438), updated request pipeline (aka [middleware](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/awslabs/smithy-go/middleware)), refactored [credential providers](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials), and [configuration loading](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config) packages.\n\nWe've also bumped the minimum supported Go version with this release. This release includes significant breaking changes to the v2 preview SDK. The updates in the `v0.25.0` release focus on refactoring and modularization of the SDK’s API clients to use the new [client design](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/438), updated request pipeline (aka [middleware](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/awslabs/smithy-go/middleware)), refactored [credential providers](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials), and [configuration loading](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config) packages.\n\nWe've also bumped the minimum supported Go version with this release. Starting with v0.25.0 the SDK requires a minimum version of Go `v1.15`.\n\nAs a part of the refactoring done to v2 preview SDK some components have not been included in this update. The following is a non exhaustive list of features that are not available.\n\n* API Paginators - [#439](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/439)\n* API Waiters - [#442](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/442)\n* Presign URL - [#794](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/794)\n* Amazon S3 Upload and Download manager - [#802](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/pull/802)\n* Amazon DynamoDB's AttributeValue marshaler, and Expression package - [#790](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/790)\n* Debug Logging - [#594](https://github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/issues/594)\n\nWe expect additional breaking changes to the v2 preview SDK in the coming releases. We expect these changes to focus on organizational, naming, and hardening the SDK's design for future feature capabilities after it is released for general availability.\n\n\n#### Relocated Packages\n\nIn this release packages within the SDK were relocated, and in some cases those packages were converted to Go modules. The following is a list of packages have were relocated.\n\n* `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/external` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config` module\n* `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/ec2metadata` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/ec2imds` module\n\nThe `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials` module contains refactored credentials providers.\n\n* `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/ec2rolecreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/ec2rolecreds`\n* `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/endpointcreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/endpointcreds`\n* `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/processcreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/processcreds`\n* `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/stscreds` => `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials/stscreds`\n\n\n#### Modularization\n\nNew modules were added to the v2 preview SDK to allow the components to be versioned independently from each other. This allows your application to depend on specific versions of an API client module, and take discrete updates from the SDK core and other API client modules as desired.\n\n* [github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config)\n* [github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials)\n* Module for each API client, e.g. [github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3)\n\n\n#### API Clients\n\nThe following is a list of the major changes to the API client modules\n\n* Removed paginators: we plan to add these back once they are implemented to integrate with the SDK's new API client design.\n* Removed waiters: we need to further investigate how the V2 SDK should expose waiters, and how their behavior should be modeled.\n* API Clients are now Go modules. When migrating to the v2 preview SDK `v0.25.0`, you'll need to add the API client's module to your application's go.mod file.\n* API parameter nested types have been moved to a `types` package within the API client's module, e.g. `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3/types` These types were moved to improve documentation and discovery of the API client, operation, and input/output types. For example Amazon S3's ListObject's operation [ListObjectOutput.Contents](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3/#ListObjectsOutput) input parameter is a slice of [types.Object](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3/types#Object).\n* The client operation method has been renamed, removing the `Request` suffix. The method now invokes the operation instead of constructing a request, which needed to be invoked separately. The operation methods were also expanded to include functional options for providing operation specific configuration, such as modifying the request pipeline.\n\n```go\nresult, err := client.Scan(context.TODO(), &dynamodb.ScanInput{\n    TableName: aws.String(\"exampleTable\"),\n}, func(o *Options) {\n    // Limit operation calls to only 1 attempt.\n    o.Retryer = retry.AddWithMaxAttempts(o.Retryer, 1)\n})\n```\n\n\n#### Configuration\n\nIn addition to the `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/external` package being made a module at `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config`, the `LoadDefaultAWSConfig` function was renamed to `LoadDefaultConfig`.\n\nThe `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/defaults` package has been removed. Its components have been migrated to the `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws` package, and `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config` module.\n\n\n#### Error Handling\n\nThe `github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/awserr` package was removed as a part of the SDK error handling refactor. The SDK now uses typed errors built around [Go v1.13](https://golang.org/doc/go1.13#error_wrapping)'s [errors.As](https://pkg.go.dev/errors#As) and [errors.Unwrap](https://pkg.go.dev/errors#Unwrap) features. All SDK error types that wrap other errors implement the `Unwrap` method. Generic v2 preview SDK errors created with `fmt.Errorf` use `%w` to wrap the underlying error.\n\nThe SDK API clients now include generated public error types for errors modeled for an API. The SDK will automatically deserialize the error response from the API into the appropriate error type. Your application should use `errors.As` to check if the returned error matches one it is interested in. Your application can also use the generic interface [smithy.APIError](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/awslabs/smithy-go/#APIError) to test if the API client's operation method returned an API error, but not check against a specific error.\n\nAPI client errors returned to the caller will use error wrapping to layer the error values. This allows underlying error types to be specific to their use case, and the SDK's more generic error types to wrap the underlying error.\n\nFor example, if an [Amazon DynamoDB](https://aws.amazon.com/dynamodb/) [Scan](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb#Scan) operation call cannot find the `TableName` requested, the error returned will contain [dynamodb.ResourceNotFoundException](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/dynamodb/types#ResourceNotFoundException). The SDK will return this error value wrapped in a couple layers, with each layer adding additional contextual information such as [ResponseError](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws/transport/http#ResponseError) for AWS HTTP response error metadata , and [smithy.OperationError](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/awslabs/smithy-go/#OperationError) for API operation call metadata.\n\n```go\nresult, err := client.Scan(context.TODO(), params)\nif err != nil {","lineStart":131,"lineEnd":210,"totalLines":245,"truncated":true}},"title":"../go/pkg/mod/github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2@v0.29.0/CHANGELOG.md","time":{"start":1782060698232,"end":1782060698247}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_0d0ac4aee698cdb5016a381699b0d08196a035f1e493aaec33"}},"id":"prt_eeb1848f7001GQaVJ0g2buONNu","sessionID":"ses_114e93f89ffeptWA2a6ndvDVDZ","messageID":"msg_eeb183fbd001496DdmXo1LhVe0"}}
